TL;DR: 针对SOC告警疲劳问题，训练了一个带链式推理（CoT）能力的三分类器，结合自动提示优化、自训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习，并额外训练校准器读取完整推理轨迹以估计判定正确的概率。系统达到82.6%测试准确率，在高置信度自动分诊操作点上，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%，且证明校准器不可或缺，微调30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）面临严重的告警疲劳问题，检测数量超过人员日均可处理能力。现有工作仅提示或微调LLM直接输出分诊标签，但没有训练模型对检测是否为真正威胁进行推理。

Method: 在真实、人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上，结合自动提示优化、自训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习，训练一个CoT推理能力的分诊分类器；同时发现CoT推理会降低标签token概率的可靠性，因此单独训练一个校准器，读取完整推理轨迹并估计判定正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%测试准确率；在高置信度自动分诊操作点上，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%（相对于直接标签LLM分类器）。未训练的置信度判断器会将高置信度召回率降为零，证明训练校准器的必要性；微调30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。

Conclusion: 训练CoT推理能力并配备专门校准器可显著提升SOC自动分诊性能，且定向训练的较小模型优于大规模通用模型，表明了针对目标任务进行训练比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [109] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对"阶段重放诊断"的核心假设（用一次性预填重建的前缀状态可等价于原始解码器状态进行续写）在Qwen2.5衍生系统的推理阶段边界处进行了审计。通过匹配的200项实验对比保留缓存与一次性预填，发现BF16下构造存在差异但准确率几乎一致；进一步通过精确到位的原生（bit-exact）桥接和双向K/V层移植证明，边界K/V缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度是行为表达的调节因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段重放诊断法被广泛用于解释大模型推理行为，但其核心假设——重建前缀后继续生成等同于从原始解码器状态继续生成——从未在真实推理场景中被严格验证。作者希望通过受控实验审计这一假设，区分"可重复性"与"状态保真性"，并厘清K/V缓存与数值精度各自在发散轨迹中的作用。

Method: 采用200项匹配实验，对比保留的实时缓存（live cache）与一次性预填（one-shot prefill）构造相同整数tokens后的续写行为。实验设计包括：(1) BF16下比较两种构造方式的标记序列与正确性标签差异，采用配对95%置信区间；(2) 固定前缀2x2因子实验，同时交叉构造方式与数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）；(3) 前瞻性桥接使逐token递增缓存与实时缓存达到bit-exact；(4) 全200项保存账本审计重现所有轨迹；(5) 在48个K/V层上进行双向移植实验，验证缓存支配性（主检查点24/24，稍后检查点43/43）。

Result: BF16下两种构造在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上存在差异，但准确率仅差1个点（配对95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]）。FP32下无任何解码差异（95% Wilson上界1.88%）。bit-exact桥接能在12/12行上消除逐token差异；双向K/V层移植使所有测试的发散续写跟随缓存供体（24/24和43/43），证明缓存因果主导轨迹，而数值精度调节其行为表达。

Conclusion: 精确token重放可以是可重复的，但不必以保留实时状态保真为前提。边界K/V缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，而其行为表达受数值精度调节。该结论为阶段重放诊断方法提供了有效性边界：在测试状态下，重现缓存即可重现行为，但研究者需注意同一token序列在不同数值精度下的解码一致性。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [110] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果行为性（causal performative）框架下的算法追索（algorithmic recourse）方法，通过结构化因果模型建模追索动作对真实标签和特征交互的影响，证明因果追索能够达到稳定均衡并减少策略性博弈，在信用卡等高风险场景中优于标准经验风险最小化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法只关注翻转模型预测，忽略了推荐动作是否真正改善个体资格或仅仅让个体策略性博弈分类器。这导致部署的追索策略会引发行为响应，降低预测准确率，并在模型重训练后失效。

Method: 构建因果行为性框架，将追索动作通过结构化因果模型传播，捕获特征间交互及其对真实标签的影响；分析了该因果响应导致的非凸优化问题，刻画了行为性稳定解存在及可通过简单迭代动力学高效计算的条件。

Result: 实验表明，忽略因果结构的追索策略会引发大且错位的行为响应，而因果追索能达到稳定均衡并减少博弈激励；在半合成和真实信用数据集上始终优于标准经验风险最小化，减少了因策略性行为导致分布偏移所需的重复模型重训练。

Conclusion: 因果感知的算法追索能够通过建模行为响应与因果机制，实现更稳健、更少需要重训练的追索策略，有效缓解策略性博弈导致的性能退化问题。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [111] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了同一图上节点分类（NC）和链接预测（LP）之间的跨任务迁移问题，提出了无泄漏的评估协议，发现NC→LP在同质图上始终有益，而LP→NC仅在结构主导场景下可靠，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）来量化联合效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界图数据通常同时支持多个预测任务（节点分类和链接预测），理论上可以复用监督信号实现跨任务迁移。然而现有评估方法在数据划分、观测图假设和负采样规则上不一致，导致关于同图跨任务迁移的结论不可靠，需要一个规范化的协议来严格评估NC-LP迁移的实际效果。

Method: 提出无泄漏的NC-LP迁移评估协议：固定节点和边划分，使用排除被评估边的共享消息传递图，并采用固定负样本进行LP。在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上系统评估NC→LP和LP→NC两个方向的迁移性能，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）衡量共享编码器同时服务两个任务时的联合效用。

Result: 迁移具有强烈的方向性和可预测性：NC→LP在同质图上始终有益；LP→NC在朴素表示复用下不稳定甚至可能降低准确率，仅在结构主导（LP容易但NC未饱和）场景下可靠为正；同质性等简单数据集统计特征可用于指导机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: NC-LP跨任务迁移并非对称有效，其方向性取决于图结构特征（尤其是同质性）。通过数据集统计量可以预判迁移机制的选择，CTS分数为共享编码器的双任务联合部署提供了量化评估工具。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [112] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APO框架，一种完全无监督的对齐方法，用于3D原子结构预测。它通过双重奖励机制（特征值分解增强主导潜在结构模式 + 热力学稳定性约束）替代传统的有监督偏好学习，在晶体和抗体结构预测上超越全监督基线，并提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的flow-matching模型（如FlowDPO）依赖与ground-truth坐标的有监督偏好学习，但在实验数据稀缺的场景（如新晶体相或de novo蛋白）中获取标注成本过高，形成瓶颈。因此需要开发无需真实参考结构的无监督对齐框架。

Method: APO将group-relative policy optimization（GRPO）适配到3D原子环境。其核心是双重奖励机制：(i) 通过样本相似性的特征值分解（eigen-decomposition）增强策略的主导潜在结构模式；(ii) 热力学稳定性约束奖励。模型通过在采样组内部识别物理上合理的构象实现自校正。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测基准上，APO一致超越全监督基线，在匹配率和结构保真度上达到新的SOTA。此外，APO有效地拉直（straighten）了概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 内在的物理一致性可以比噪声较大的有监督坐标匹配更好地指导模型对齐，这表明无监督物理约束是数据稀缺场景下结构预测的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [113] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD，将原始的on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）泛化为一个可控正则化参数β家族，推导出其最优策略为参考策略与特权教师之间的几何插值，并利用闭环解构造蒸馏目标，通过token级logits混合高效近似昂贵的策略优化，在数学推理基准上优于vanilla OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vanilla OPSD在实践中表现脆弱，需要大量工程调试才能使其可靠工作。论文识别出这一困难的根源在于OPSD恰好是更广泛的策略优化家族中β=1的特例，缺乏可控的正则化参数来权衡参考策略锚定与教师指导之间的平衡。

Method: 将OPSD泛化为β-OPSD（β为KL散度惩罚权重），推导出最优策略为参考策略与特权教师的几何插值形式。不直接优化高方差、昂贵的RL目标，而是利用该目标的闭环解构造蒸馏目标——通过混合参考策略与教师策略的token级logits来实现不同β值对应的插值目标。同时引入return-to-go信用分配使token更新与序列级目标对齐。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，β-OPSD在优化稳定性和下游推理性能上均一致优于vanilla OPSD。

Conclusion: 本文提供了从自蒸馏到策略优化之间的一条原理性路径，在不牺牲OPSD实用效率的前提下，将β从隐含的固定值（1）转变为可控的正则化超参数，实现了从自蒸馏到策略优化的可解释过渡。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [114] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAISEN是一个五阶段审计管线，在16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴及3个预定交叉组的合成基准上被评估至失效点。研究揭示四项关键发现：显著性指标受最小可检测效应影响；组间阈值优化可靠改善EOD而Platt缩放平均效果近零；机制诊断在代理误设下完全失效且无失败信号；CUSUM告警阈值在不同队列间不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在总体表现上强劲但在患者子群间存在显著误差差异。已有的审计管线虽被提出以捕捉此类问题，但其各个组件很少被压力测试，导致审计中哪些部分可信、在何种条件下可信尚不清楚。

Method: 提出KAISEN五阶段审计管线，涵盖子群分层、差异度量、机制诊断、事后缓解与漂移监控。在合成基准上系统评估至失效点，利用已知ground truth验证各组件在受控与真实条件下的行为边界。合成基准包含16个疾病任务、15个Healthy People 2030社会决定因素轴及3个预定交叉组。

Result: (i)显著性计数与原始EOD秩相关rho=0.56，按最小可检测效应标准化后升至0.78；(ii)组间阈值优化在48/48次held-out运行中降低EOD（配对delta=-0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]），而Platt缩放仅19/48次改善且均值效应近零（审计应报告方差而非均值）；(iii)机制诊断在144/144个受控案例中正确分类，但在代理误设下48个模型驱动案例全部无法恢复且无失败信号；(iv)在参考阈值下，27个误报和8次漏报中的7次来自不同种子（chi-squared p=0.002），阈值不可跨队列迁移。

Conclusion: 审计管线的各组件可靠性与条件性差异显著：需关注最小可检测效应对显著性度量的影响；正确度量应当报告缓解方法的方差而非平均效应；机制诊断在代理误设下静默失效需要专门验证；漂移监控阈值必须针对具体队列重新校准。所有发现均基于合成数据，具有已知ground truth，不构成临床有效性证据。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [OwlPath: Lossless Knowledge Compression for LLM Bug Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27249)
*Bo Zhang,Ren Pan,Huan Chen,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OwlPath是一个基于OWL2本体推理层的代码结构检索工具，构建在CodeGraph之上，通过将代码编码为OWL2本体并使用SPARQL属性路径查询来实现无损知识压缩，解决LLM软件工程代理在有限上下文窗口下的多跳依赖检索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM软件工程代理受限于约100K tokens的上下文窗口，需要存储结构相关的代码子集来修复bug。传统检索模型将代码视为纯文本，导致代理无法高效解析多跳依赖关系（如子类链、传递调用者和接口实现），只能通过缓慢的试错方式解决。

Method: OwlPath采用两个互补模块：(1) 传递闭包引擎，通过单一SPARQL属性路径查询获取所有结构关联符号，捕获字符串匹配遗漏的多跳关系；(2) OWL软件知识映射（OWL-SKM），预计算约3KB的紧凑摘要（包含模块树、核心API和问题关联符号），引导代理在首次查询时就定位到目标模块。基于tree-sitter解析，支持多语言仓库（Python、JavaScript、TypeScript、Go等），将语言特定语义编码为统一的OWL2本体。

Result: 在18个SWE-bench Pro实例上，OwlPath获得68.4%的严格应用率（CodeGraph基线为66.7%），token使用减少28.8%，运行时间减少39.5%。在67个实例的离线检索测试中，召回率提升2.06倍（0.464 vs 0.226），命中率达到88.1%（CodeGraph为59.7%）。在37个结构检索问题的基准上，召回率从4.4%提升至28.8%，传递调用者和接口任务的准确率达到69-80%。

Conclusion: OwlPath通过OWL2本体推理层有效解决了代码结构检索中的多跳依赖问题，显著提升了检索召回率和命中率，同时大幅降低了token使用量和运行时间，验证了基于知识图谱的本体推理方法在代码智能领域的高效性和可行性。

Abstract: LLM-based software engineering agents are constrained by limited context windows: roughly 100K tokens must store structurally relevant code  subsets to resolve bugs. Standard retrieval models treat code as plain text, forcing agents to resolve multi-hop dependencies including subclass  chains, transitive callers and interface implementations through slow trial and error. We tackle this limitation with lossless knowledge  compression, encoding source code into an OWL2 ontology to answer structural queries using minimal relevant code fragments.
  We present OwlPath, an OWL2 reasoning layer atop CodeGraph, a widely used code intelligence platform with 500K+ GitHub stars, offering a unified  CLI for structural code retrieval. Powered by tree-sitter parsing, OwlPath supports multi-language repositories (Python, JavaScript,  TypeScript, Go, etc.) and encodes language-specific semantics into a unified OWL2 ontology. It adopts two complementary modules. First, a  transitive-closure engine fetches all structurally linked symbols via single SPARQL property-path queries, capturing multi-hop relations missed  by string matching. Second, the OWL Software Knowledge Map (OWL-SKM) precomputes a compact 3KB summary with module trees, core APIs and  issue-related symbols, directing agents to target modules in the first query.
  Evaluated on 18 SWE-bench Pro instances, OwlPath obtains a 68.4% strict-apply rate versus 66.7% for the CodeGraph baseline, cutting token usage  by 28.8% and runtime by 39.5%. In offline retrieval tests over 67 instances, OwlPath improves recall 2.06 times (0.464 vs 0.226) and reaches  88.1% hit rate compared to CodeGraph's 59.7%. On a 37-question structural retrieval benchmark, recall rises from 4.4% to 28.8%, with 69-80%  accuracy on transitive caller and interface tasks.

</details>


### [116] [Do Context Files Help Coding Agents? A Two-Agent Ablation Study on Real Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27250)
*Prakhar Khatri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验（2个前沿AI编码代理、3个仓库的17个真实任务、288次评估运行），发现持久上下文文件（AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md）并不会显著提升编码代理的正确率（差异限制在10-15pp以内）。失败模式分类表明代理失败源于实现技能（特性设计、模式选择、精确接线）而非缺失仓库知识；操作探针证实真实上下文文件无法将近通过转化为通过。任务难度的边界是代理特异的（Spearman rho=0.75），解释了先前研究结论矛盾的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久上下文文件（如AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md）已成为引导AI编码代理的标准实践，但关于其有效性的证据相互矛盾。作者希望通过受控实验厘清上下文注入策略到底能否提升代理性能，并解释已有研究结论不一致的根源。

Method: 采用对照消融方法：在Claude Code和Codex两个前沿代理上，对17个来自3个仓库的真实任务（15个共享+2个Codex独有）、共288次评估运行进行黄金测试评估。同时进行失败模式分类（triage）和操作探针（manipulation probe）实验，并计算代理间任务难度排名的Spearman相关系数。

Result: （1）上下文策略在两个代理上均未可测量地提升正确率（等价检验将差异上界限制在10-15pp内）；（2）代理失败主要源于实现技能不足，而非缺乏上下文文件能够提供的仓库知识；（3）真实AGENTS.md无法将接近通过的结果转化为通过；（4）两个代理的边界任务难度仅中度相关（Spearman rho=0.75），说明单一代理研究可能抽样自不同代理的有效区分区间。

Conclusion: 持久上下文文件对编码代理正确率的提升效果有限，代理瓶颈在于实现能力而非知识供给。先前研究中关于上下文文件有效性的矛盾结论可能源于任务选择偏差——不同研究在设计任务时落在了不同代理的可区分难度区间。作者开源了全部代码、数据与分析以支持复现。

Abstract: Persistent context files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md) are standard practice for guiding AI coding agents, yet evidence for their effectiveness is contradictory. We present a controlled ablation of context-injection strategy across two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex), 17 real tasks from 3 repositories (15 shared + 2 Codex-only), and 288 evaluated runs with gold-test evaluation. Context strategy does not measurably move correctness on either agent (bounded to <=10-15pp via equivalence testing). A failure-mode triage reveals why: agents fail on implementation skill---feature design, pattern selection, exact wiring---not missing repository knowledge that a context file could supply; a manipulation probe confirms the real AGENTS.md never converts a near-miss to a pass on either agent. We further show that borderline task difficulty is agent-specific (Spearman rho=0.75), offering a candidate explanation for prior contradictions: single-agent studies draw tasks from different agents' informative bands. We release all code, data, and analysis.

</details>


### [117] [AgentS4D: Benchmarking Runtime Risks across the Execution Lifecycle of LLM-Based Workspace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27294)
*Jiajun Zhou,Zhaoxuan Ke,Jihang Ye,Xuanze Chen,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentS4D，一个用于LLM工作区代理生命周期级运行时安全评估的沙箱基准测试。它通过四维运行时安全框架（六种风险入口、六种诱导策略、九种目标危害、七个生命周期检查点）构建了328个风险注入案例，评估了20种组合配置（4种工作台×5种LLM后端）共6,560次运行。结果显示68%的运行触发了不安全信号，66.22%的运行不安全但已完成，表明任务完成不能证明运行时安全，且需在多样化风险条件下评估完整代理配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工作区代理的安全评估缺少统一框架来系统化地说明风险从何处进入、如何诱导不安全行为、针对哪些危害以及支持性证据在执行过程中出现在哪里。已有基准虽有可执行安全测试和轨迹感知验证，但无法提供全生命周期的统一安全分析视角。

Method: 提出AgentS4D沙箱基准测试，采用四维运行时安全框架：六种风险入口源、六种诱导策略、九种目标危害用于指导案例构建，并使用七个生命周期检查点组织运行后证据。在328个风险注入案例上，评估4种工作台（Hermes、OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex）与5种LLM后端（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、MiniMax-M3、Qwen3.7-Plus）的全部20种组合，共完成6,560次沙箱运行。

Result: 4,461次运行（68.0%）触发预设的不安全信号。代理系统的安全行为同时取决于工作台-LLM配对方式和风险引入方式；相同诱导策略通过不同风险载体传播时，代理系统表现出显著不同的安全行为；相同目标危害通过不同载体和策略实现时响应也不同。此外4,344次运行（66.22%）不安全但执行完成，说明任务完成不等同于运行时安全。

Conclusion: 评估应在多样化的风险条件下检查完整的代理配置（工作台+LLM的组合），并在执行全程保留证据；仅测试单一形式的风险会掩盖重要安全弱点，需要生命周期级别的综合安全评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based workspace agents execute stateful, multi-step workflows across heterogeneous resources, external tools, and persistent state. Their safety must therefore be assessed from actions, side effects, and state changes throughout execution. Although recent benchmarks have advanced executable safety testing and trajectory-aware verification, they rarely provide a unified account of where risks enter, how they elicit unsafe behavior, which harms they target, and where supporting evidence appears during execution. We introduce AgentS4D, a sandboxed benchmark for lifecycle-wide runtime safety evaluation. Its four-dimensional runtime-safety framework uses six risk-entry sources, six induction strategies, and nine target harms to guide case construction, while seven lifecycle checkpoints organize post-run evidence. AgentS4D contains 328 risk-injected cases. We evaluate all 20 combinations of four harnesses (Hermes, OpenClaw, Claude Code, and Codex) and five LLM backends (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, MiniMax-M3, and Qwen3.7-Plus) on these cases, yielding 6,560 runs. Overall, 4,461 runs (68.0%) trigger prespecified unsafe signals. Across the 20 configurations, the observed safety of an agent system varies with both its harness-LLM pairing and how risk is introduced. Agent systems exhibit markedly different safety behavior when the same induction strategy reaches them through different risk carriers. They also respond differently to the same target harm when it is realized through different carriers and strategies. Moreover, 4,344 runs (66.22% overall) are unsafe yet complete. Thus, task completion cannot establish runtime safety, and testing only one form of a risk can conceal important weaknesses. Evaluations should examine complete agent configurations across diverse risk conditions and retain evidence throughout execution.

</details>


### [118] [A Taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27302)
*Anastasia Mavridou,Hazel M. Taylor,Sandy Lozito,Louise A. Dennis,Michael Fisher,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机团队协作（HRT）需求的分类体系，通过分析361条来自14个跨领域来源的需求构建了两级层次分类结构（6个高层类别、21个低层子类别），并通过专家评估和独立语料库验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统越来越多地部署在安全关键和任务关键领域，人类与机器人需要作为团队协作完成复杂任务。然而，现有的人机团队协作需求分散在不同来源中，缺乏统一的框架来应对协作任务的复杂性，因此需要构建一个统一的需求分类体系。

Method: 从学术文献、工业文献、标准和监管指南中提取了361条需求构建语料库；通过迭代分类和精化，开发出一个两级的层次化分类体系，包含6个高层类别和21个低层子类别；随后通过5位领域专家的评估以及在一个独立构建的、涵盖6个HRT领域、来自19个来源的448条需求语料库上进行实用性演示来验证该分类体系。

Result: 成功构建了两级层次的HRT需求分类体系，涵盖信息提供、关系控制、决策支持、安全机制、性能监控和基础系统能力6个高层类别及21个低层子类别。通过专家评估和独立语料库（448条需求、19个来源、6个领域）的效用演示验证了该分类体系的适用性。

Conclusion: 该分类体系填补了人机团队协作需求统一框架的空白，为自主系统的需求工程提供了跨领域的结构化指导，有助于在安全关键和任务关键领域更好地实现人类与机器人的协同工作。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in safety- and mission-critical domains where humans and robots must operate as a team to complete complex tasks. Existing requirements for Human-Robot teamwork remain fragmented across disparate sources, with no unified framework that addresses complexities of collaborative Human-Robot tasks. We address this gap by presenting a taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork (HRT) requirements derived from analysis of (academic and industrial) literature, standards and regulatory guidance. We extracted a construction corpus of 361 requirements from 14 cross-domain sources. Through iterative classification and refinement, we develop a two-level hierarchical taxonomy comprising 6 high-level categories and 21 low-level subcategories that distinguish information provision, relational control, decision support, safety mechanisms, performance monitoring, and foundational system capabilities. We validate the taxonomy through expert evaluation with 5 domain specialists and a utility demonstration on an independently assembled corpus of 448 requirements drawn from 19 sources spanning six HRT domains.

</details>


### [119] [SIGIL: Compiling Agent Skills into Typed Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27309)
*Jayanaka Dantanarayana,Savini Kashmira,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill Compilation（技能编译）方法，通过SIGIL系统将散文式技能编译为可执行harness，采用AG-IR（类型化Agentic中间表示）分离模型认知与代码机制。实验表明，编译后的harness在30个技能和两代模型上执行88%的要求步骤（对比散文式agent的56%），完整流程完成率提升2.3倍，token消耗降低42%，且保证与模型代际无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI集成agent通过加载到上下文中的散文式技能文件获取能力，由工具调用循环执行。然而技能仅被描述给运行时而从未编码进系统，导致模型每次运行都需要重新推导控制流，可能跳过强制验证步骤。实验显示散文式agent仅执行自身技能要求的56%步骤。手工编写harness虽能解决此问题，但过程繁琐且舍弃了技能成功的创作界面。

Method: SIGIL系统实现Skill Compilation，将散文式技能编译为可执行harness。核心是AG-IR（typed agentic intermediate representation），一种类型化agentic中间表示，将模型拥有的认知与代码拥有的机制相分离。编译后的harness将过程转化为程序结构，确保控制流的执行确定性。

Result: 编译后的harness执行86%的要求步骤（散文式为56%），完整过程完成频率提升2.3倍，token使用量降为0.58倍。关键的是保证与模型无关：在两种模型代际上harness均保持在86%，而散文式在56%到68%之间波动。同时，产物能通过输出检查。

Conclusion: Skill Compilation通过将散文技能编译为程序结构化的harness，既保留了技能的创作界面优势，又显著提升了执行流程的完整性、可靠性、效率与跨模型一致性，为AI agent技能执行提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: AI-Integrated agents increasingly acquire capability from skills: prose procedure files loaded into a model's context and run by a tool-calling loop. A skill is described to the runtime but never encoded in it, so the model re-derives its control flow on every run and may skip mandated verification. Across 30 skills and two model generations, a prose agent performs only 56% of the steps its own skill mandates, while producing artifacts that pass output checks. The remedy is known: write a harness, in which the procedure is program structure. However, hand-writing harnesses is tedious and discards the authoring surface that made skills succeed. To address this limitation, we introduce Skill Compilation, realized in SIGIL, which compiles a prose skill into an executable harness. At its center is AG-IR, a typed agentic intermediate representation separating model-owned cognition from code-owned mechanism. Compiled harnesses perform 86% of mandated steps, complete the full procedure 2.3x as often, and require 0.58x the tokens. Notably, the guarantee is model-independent: the harness holds at 86% across two model generations while prose swings from 56% to 68%.

</details>


### [120] [TrustChain-Review: A Risk-Adaptive Blockchain and Game-Theoretic Framework for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27310)
*Mohammad Naserameri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrustChain-Review框架，结合可验证证据、策略性激励和风险敏感治理来支持更可信的代码审查，通过区块链证据层、三玩家博弈模型和自适应治理规则，在模拟实验中比较了六种治理配置，发现全证据配置在声誉、信任和检测方面最强但成本最高，而风险自适应配置在成本效益上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助软件开发加速了编码和审查，但也使问责变得更困难：开发者可能提交未充分验证的代码，审查者可能以有限的检查批准更改，且集中的声誉记录难以审计。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：基于区块链的证据层、涉及开发者/审查者/平台的三玩家博弈论模型，以及一条规则——当预期收益证明其成本合理时应用更强的治理。评估采用受Diff Quality Estimation数据集校准的受控模拟，比较六种治理配置，进行30次独立运行，衡量声誉准确性、信任收敛、恶意审查检测、表面审查检测、平台净效用、治理成本和成本效益。

Result: 全证据配置在声誉、信任和检测结果方面最强，但治理成本最高。风险自适应配置通过选择性应用更强控制来降低成本并提高成本效益，但其信任和检测结果低于全证据设置。

Conclusion: 强大的基于证据的治理最适合高风险或审计敏感的变化；对于常规或较低风险的贡献，选择性治理在可信度与运营成本之间提供了更实用的平衡。

Abstract: Context: AI-assisted software development can speed up coding and review, but it also makes accountability harder to establish. Developers may submit insufficiently verified code, reviewers may approve changes with limited inspection, and centralized reputation records may be difficult to audit. Objectives: This study introduces TrustChain-Review, a framework that combines verifiable evidence, strategic incentives, and risk-sensitive governance to support more trustworthy code review. Methods: The framework includes a blockchain-based evidence layer, a three-player game-theoretic model for developers, reviewers, and the platform, and a rule that applies stronger governance when the expected benefit justifies its cost. The evaluation uses a controlled simulation calibrated with the Diff Quality Estimation dataset. Six governance configurations are compared over 30 independent runs using reputation accuracy, trust convergence, malicious-review detection, superficial-review detection, net platform utility, governance cost, and cost-efficiency. Results: The full-evidence configuration produces the strongest reputation, trust, and detection results, but it also has the highest governance cost. The risk-adaptive configuration lowers this cost and improves cost-efficiency by applying stronger controls selectively, although its trust and detection results are lower than those of the full-evidence setting. Conclusion: Strong evidence-based governance is most appropriate for high-risk or audit-sensitive changes. For routine or lower-risk contributions, selective governance offers a more practical balance between trustworthiness and operational cost.

</details>


### [121] [PROGRESS: Property-Guided Regression Search for Semantic Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27359)
*Davis Tocheuk Mo,Noshin Ulfat,Matthew B. Dwyer,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PROGRESS是一个将意图驱动的属性测试与覆盖率引导的搜索式进化测试生成相结合的方法，通过语言模型生成jqwik属性、扩展DynaMOSA搜索算法，在保持结构覆盖率的同时检测回归测试无法发现的缺陷。在25个大型Java系统上，检测到328/562个当前版本bug（58%），并满足70/150个难以到达属性的前置条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的回归测试生成能有效探索复杂程序结构并获得高结构覆盖率，但其测试预言（oracle）来源于被测系统本身，已存在的故障会被记录为预期行为而非被暴露。属性测试提供独立的语义预言，但依赖高质量属性且对深入状态或满足选择性前置条件缺乏指导。两种方法各有局限，需要将它们集成以取长补短。

Method: PROGRESS包含三个核心组件：(1)提取携带意图的代码上下文，通过语言模型管道生成可执行的jqwik属性，同时限制实现细节泄漏；(2)扩展EvoSuite的DynaMOSA算法，为每个属性引入搜索目标和属性感知的适应度函数，奖励通过前置条件的进度并优先考虑伪造执行；(3)绑定属性参数并利用jqwik提供的生成器，将量化输入连接到演化的测试序列中，引导生成过程同时朝向覆盖率和缺陷检测目标。

Result: 在25个大型Java系统上评估，PROGRESS检测到328/562个当前版本bug（58%），而回归测试生成检测到0个；对于150个难以到达的属性，PROGRESS满足70个的前置条件，而独立jqwik仅满足18个。消融实验表明文档和调用者/被调用者上下文是生成有效可执行属性的关键。PROGRESS在保持结构探索的同时暴露了回归派生断言所遗漏的故障。

Conclusion: PROGRESS成功地将属性测试的独立语义预言与覆盖率引导的搜索式测试生成相结合，在维持结构覆盖率的同时显著提升了缺陷检测能力，并发布了完整的工件包供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Search-based regression-test generation effectively explores complex program structures, yielding high structural coverage, but its oracles are derived from the system under test: faults already present are recorded as expected behavior rather than exposed. Property-based testing offers independent semantic oracles, but depends on high-quality properties and gives little guidance for reaching deep states or satisfying selective preconditions.
  We present PROGRESS (PROperty-Guided REgression Search for Semantic Falsification), integrating intent-driven properties into coverage-guided, search-based evolutionary test generation to reach deep program states and detect violations of intended behavior. PROGRESS (1) extracts intent-bearing code context and uses a language-model pipeline to generate executable jqwik properties while limiting implementation leakage; (2) extends EvoSuite's DynaMOSA with a search objective and property-aware fitness function per property, rewarding progress through preconditions and prioritizing falsifying executions; and (3) binds property parameters and uses jqwik-provided generators to connect quantified inputs to evolving test sequences, steering generation toward coverage and bug-detection goals.
  We evaluate PROGRESS on 25 large-scale Java systems against regression-test generation, standalone property-based testing, and context ablations. PROGRESS detects 328/562 current-version bugs (58%) versus none for regression-test generation, and satisfies all preconditions for 70/150 hard-to-reach properties versus 18 for standalone jqwik. Ablations show documentation and caller/callee context are key to generating valid executable properties. PROGRESS preserves structural exploration while exposing faults missed by regression-derived assertions; we release a comprehensive artifact package.

</details>


### [122] [From Backlog Items to Security Guidance: Towards Continuous Security Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27374)
*Ignacio García Núñez,Florian Angermeir,Fabiola Moyón Constante*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自然语言处理（NLP）的待办事项（backlog）增强系统，用于检测安全相关的待办事项并将其链接到适用的安全需求。系统结合安全相关性分类器与检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，在高度受监管领域的大型企业中进行开发与评估，包含三项贡献：带标注的数据集、召回导向的分类器以及初步评估的RAG流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域进行持续软件工程时，团队需在整个开发生命周期中应对安全问题，但在待办事项中明确表达安全需求仍然困难。工程师需从简短的自由文本中推断待办事项的安全相关性，且常常缺乏及时的需求指导，这导致安全需求容易被遗漏或引入过晚。

Method: 提出一个结合两部分的系统：(1) 安全相关性分类器，用于识别安全相关的待办事项；(2) 基于安全需求文档的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，将识别出的待办事项链接到相关安全需求。研究还构建了一个由九位安全从业者标注的288条待办事项数据集，并对分类器进行分布内和零样本基准测试，同时对RAG流水线进行初步工业评估。

Result: 分类器在分布内达到F2=0.774，在五个既有基准上零样本平均G-measure约为0.65，匹敌或超越大多数已发表的经典机器学习与开源GPT基线。RAG流水线检索出的24个条款中，12个被评分为至少4/5的相关性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为NLP驱动的待办事项增强能够支持工程师在开发过程早期识别安全需求提供了初步证据，旨在通过主动引入安全需求促进持续软件工程中的持续安全合规。

Abstract: Continuous software engineering in regulated domains requires engineering teams to address security throughout the development lifecycle. Yet making security requirements explicit in backlog items is still problematic. Engineers must instead infer security relevance of backlog items from brief, free-form descriptions and often lack timely guidance on applicable requirements. We present an NLP-based backlog enrichment system that detects security-relevant backlog items and links them to relevant security requirements. The approach combines a security-relevance classifier with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline over security requirements documents. The approach was developed and evaluated in the context of a large enterprise in highly regulated domains. We present three contributions. First, we release a dataset of 288 backlog items labeled for security relevance by nine security practitioners, with substantial agreement (Fleiss' $κ=0.787$). Second, a recall-oriented classifier achieving $F2=0.774$ in-distribution and mean zero-shot G-measure $\approx 0.65$ across five established benchmarks, matching or outperforming most published classical-ML and open-source GPT baselines. Third, we preliminarily evaluated a four-stage security requirements document-grounded RAG pipeline with two practitioners on industrial backlogs using company-internal security policies and CIS Benchmarks. Of the retrieved 24 clauses, 12 were rated at least 4/5 for relevance. Our findings provide first indicators that NLP-based product backlog enrichment can support engineers in identifying security requirements early in the development process. With this work we aim to facilitate continuous security compliance through proactive introduction of security requirements in continuous software engineering.

</details>


### [123] [SWE-NFI: Studying and Benchmarking Coding Agents for Non-Functional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27409)
*Pengyu Xue,He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Junkai Chen,Haonan Zhang,Boyuan Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SWE-NFI基准，用于评估编码智能体在保持行为不变的前提下进行非功能性改进（NFI）的能力。基准包含188个来自真实Python开源项目合并PR的任务，将NFI操作化为92条可执行规则，并结合功能正确性测试与规则评估。实验表明即使最佳智能体的功能正确率达70%，其NFI能力仍显著落后于人类开发者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准主要评估功能正确性，但现实软件开发中开发者持续进行不改变可观察行为的非功能性改进。缺乏专门评估编码智能体此类能力的基准，导致智能体在NFI方面的能力未被充分探索。

Method: 从真实开源Python项目的合并PR中构建188个NFI任务；将开发者导向的NFI操作化为92条可执行规则；开发结合功能正确性测试与基于规则的NFI评估套件；评估商业和开源SOTA编码智能体。

Result: 最佳智能体功能正确率70.0%，但所有被测智能体在整体NFI能力上均落后于人类开发者。结构性代码改进方面差距尤为明显：智能体NFI分数范围0.0-1.3，人类参考为1.5。

Conclusion: SWE-NFI为评估和推进编码智能体超越功能正确性提供了可复现的基础，揭示了当前编码智能体在非功能性改进能力上的明显短板。

Abstract: Although coding agents have achieved impressive performance on correctness-oriented benchmarks, their ability to make behavior-preserving non-functional improvements (NFIs) remains underexplored. In real-world software development, developers continuously improve software quality without changing observable behavior, yet existing benchmarks primarily evaluate functional correctness and provide limited support for assessing these non-functional improvements.
  In this paper, we present SWE-NFI, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on NFIs beyond functional correctness. Our benchmark contains 188 tasks constructed from real merged pull requests in open-source Python projects. We operationalize developer-oriented NFIs into 92 executable rules and develop a comprehensive evaluation suite that combines functional correctness testing with rule-based NFI evaluation.
  We evaluate state-of-the-art commercial and open-source coding agents. Although the best-performing agent achieves a 70.0\% functional correctness rate, all evaluated agents generally fall short of human developers in overall NFI capability. The gap is particularly evident for structural code improvements, where agents' NFI scores range from 0.0 to 1.3, compared with 1.5 for the human reference. Our benchmark and findings provide a reproducible foundation for evaluating and advancing coding agents beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [124] [LimICE: Integrating LLM into ICE Framework for Efficient Loop Invariant Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27606)
*Kai Fan,ShiWen Yu,GuangSheng Fan,HaoAng Chi,WanWei Liu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Incremental ICE，一种将IC3的增量思想融入ICE框架的循环不变量学习框架，并通过LimICE工具利用LLM生成有序引理序列来实现增量合成，在367个线性和50个非线性基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环不变量合成是程序验证中的基础问题，但由于固有的不可判定性而极具挑战。现有的基于机器学习的循环不变量生成方法大多采用整体式（monolithic）方法，由于无法严格约束学习过程，这些方法在解决复杂问题时难以同时考虑所有必要条件并生成完整的不变量。事实上，循环不变量往往是由有序的引理序列构成，而不是单一的公式，这促使研究者探索增量式合成方法。

Method: 本文提出Incremental ICE框架，将IC3的增量哲学融入通用的ICE不变量学习框架中。具体方法包括：1）定义引理特定的学习目标（lemma-specific learning objective）；2）引入反例过滤机制以实现可靠的增量学习；3）在该框架下实例化工具LimICE，利用LLM生成有序引理序列，并以ICE-DT作为后备机制补充引理序列。

Result: 在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上，LimICE分别求解了349个线性问题（平均15.2秒）和47个非线性问题（平均8.8秒）。与最先进的基于LLM的基线相比，在线性和非线性基准上多解决12-24%的实例，同时运行速度快36-63%。与强非LLM基线相比，LimICE在线性和非线性基准上分别多解决至少86个和27个实例。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过将增量思想引入不变量学习框架，并利用LLM生成有序引理序列，可以有效提升循环不变量合成的效率和完整性，超越现有的基于LLM和非LLM的方法。

Abstract: Loop invariant synthesis is a fundamental problem in program verification, yet the inherent undecidability makes it highly challenging. Recent studies have increasingly employed various machine learning techniques to generate loop invariants. However, most of these methods adopt a monolithic approach. Due to the inability to strictly constrain the learning process, learning-based methods struggle to simultaneously consider all necessary conditions and generate complete invariants when tackling complex problems. In fact, a loop invariant is often an ordered sequence of lemmas, rather than a single invariant formula. This motivates us to propose Incremental ICE, a novel learning framework for incremental synthesis. Our framework integrates the incremental philosophy of IC3 into the general invariant learning framework ICE. By defining a lemma-specific learning objective and introducing a counterexample filtering mechanism, we can achieve sound incremental learning. Under this framework, we instantiate a loop invariant synthesis tool, LimICE, which leverages LLMs to generate the ordered sequence of lemmas and incorporates ICE-DT as a fallback mechanism to complement the lemma sequence. Experiments on 367 linear benchmarks and 50 nonlinear benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. LimICE solves 349 (out of 367) linear problems on an average of 15.2 seconds and 47 (out of 50) nonlinear problems on an average of 8.8 seconds. Compared to the state-of-the-art LLM-based baseline, our approach solves 12-24% more instances while running 36-63% faster across linear and nonlinear benchmarks. LimICE also consistently outperforms strong non-LLM baselines and solves at least 86 and 27 additional instances on the linear and nonlinear benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [125] [Not as Sweet by Another Name: An Empirical Study of Format Robustness in LLM Document Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27648)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Xianyun Cheng,Tianlin Li,Yuwei Zheng,Yue Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个格式感知的元变形测试框架，系统评估端到端LLM文档工作流在不同文档格式下的鲁棒性。通过48,000次工作流执行实验发现，仅切换文档格式即可导致准确率下降高达53.63%，并引发超过41%实例的决策漂移；同时设计了无需模型重训练的用户侧轻量缓解策略，可恢复最多44.21%的格式诱导决策漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM测试研究主要关注单一提示词字符串输入下模型和系统的鲁棒性与可靠性，但LLM驱动的软件系统正从纯文本对话演变为以文档为中心的端到端工作流，相同语义内容可通过文件上传接口以不同文档格式（如CSV）交付。文档格式变化是否会影响工作流的鲁棒行为这一问题尚未被回答。

Method: 提出格式感知的元变形测试框架，包含三条元变形关系，用于综合评估端到端LLM文档工作流的格式鲁棒性。基于该框架开展大规模实证研究，覆盖四个代表性LLM工作流、四个真实世界任务和四种文档格式，共计48,000次工作流执行。此外设计了从用户视角出发的轻量级缓解策略。

Result: 实证研究发现格式变化构成系统性且严重的威胁：仅切换文档格式即可使准确率下降最多53.63%，并在超过41%的实例中引发决策漂移。用户侧轻量缓解策略可在不进行模型重训练的情况下恢复最多44.21%的格式诱导决策漂移。

Conclusion: 文档格式并非中立的包装载体，而是影响LLM软件系统可靠性的关键因素。研究呼吁在真实世界高风险场景的部署中应关注相应的测试与安全保障措施，并验证了无需模型重训练的用户侧缓解策略的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-driven software systems are rapidly evolving from plain-text conversations to document-centric end-to-end workflows, where the same semantic content can be delivered in diverse document formats (e.g., CSV) through file upload interfaces. Yet existing testing work focuses on the robustness and reliability of models and systems whose input is a single prompt string, leaving a critical question unanswered: Can these document workflows maintain robust behaviors when the same content arrives in a different document format? To fill the gap, in this paper, we propose a format-aware metamorphic testing framework with three metamorphic relations to comprehensively evaluate the format robustness of end-to-end LLM document workflows. Based on this framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study spanning four representative LLM workflows, four real-world tasks, and four document formats, comprising a total of 48,000 workflow executions. Our findings reveal that format variation poses a systematic and serious threat. Merely switching formats can cause accuracy to drop by up to 53.63% and trigger decision drifts in over 41% of instances. We further design lightweight mitigation strategies from the users' perspective that recover up to 44.21% of format-induced decision drift without model retraining. Our study demonstrates that document format is not a neutral wrapper but a critical factor affecting the reliability of LLM software systems, calling for corresponding testing and safeguards in the deployment in real-world high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [Can Large Language Models Resolve Real Java Merge Conflicts? An Evaluation with a Calibrated LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27674)
*Bowen Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对合并冲突解决的规模评估难题，构建了基于LLM的generate-validate-retry求解器（仅使用推理时信号，不使用开发者答案），并提出双指标评估套件：基于G-Eval的LLM-as-judge（经人类标注校准后达到100%精确率/64.6%召回率）和确定性结构有效性检查。在292个人类标注案例的元评估中，LLM求解器在55%的真实冲突上匹配开发者解法，覆盖率公平比较下（55-59%）显著优于最强的传统工具AutoMerge（36.7%），优势几乎完全来自覆盖率而非原始准确率。同时发现LLM法官接受了5个结构性失败解析中的4个，表明结构性正确性不能委托给LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合并冲突是协作开发中的持续成本，传统结构化/半结构化合并工具经常因启发式不适用而弃权（abstain）；LLM可以为几乎所有冲突生成候选解法，但大规模评估其解析质量很困难，因为获取每个模型输出的人类偏好判断不可扩展。作者希望同时解决"构建更好的LLM冲突求解器"和"在无人类标注条件下可靠评估LLM解析质量"两个问题。

Method: 1) 构建LLM求解器：采用generate-validate-retry智能体架构，仅使用推理时信号（冲突标记、Java解析器、重复声明检查），从不接触开发者的真实答案。2) 构建双指标评估套件：a) developer-match LLM-as-judge（G-Eval指标实现），在使用前用ConflictBench的人类标签校准；b) 确定性结构有效性检查（不使用LLM）。3) 在ConflictBench的真实Java合并冲突上进行元评估，比较LLM与传统工具（如AutoMerge）的表现。

Result: 1) 在292个人类标注案例上，LLM法官达到100%精确率（零误接受）和64.6%召回率，因此每个接受都是可信的，下游比率都是保守下界。2) 在该验证过的法官下，LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突上匹配开发者解法（保守底线）。3) 覆盖率公平比较下，LLM（55-59%）比传统最强工具AutoMerge（36.7%）高出18-22个百分点；优势几乎完全来自覆盖率，因为传统工具在20-90%的冲突上弃权，而LLM在强制解析下从不弃权。4) LLM法官接受了5个未通过确定性结构检查的解析中的4个，证明结构性正确性不能委托给LLM判断。

Conclusion: LLM求解器在合并冲突解决上显著优于传统工具，优势主要来自覆盖率（从不弃权）而非准确率差异。LLM-as-judge通过校准可以达到100%精确率，适合大规模自动评估，但结构性正确性必须保留确定性检查手段，不能完全依赖LLM判断。该工作展示了可扩展、可信赖的LLM冲突解析评估范式。

Abstract: Merge conflicts are a recurring cost of collaborative software development, and the traditional structured and semi-structured merge tools that address them frequently abstain: when their heuristics do not apply, they leave the conflict unresolved. Large language models (LLMs) can instead produce a candidate resolution for almost any conflict, but measuring whether those resolutions are actually good at scale is hard, because obtaining human desirability judgments for every model output does not scale. We study both problems together on real Java merge conflicts from ConflictBench. We first build an LLM solver as a generate-validate-retry agent that uses only inference-time signals (conflict markers, a Java parser, and duplicate-declaration checks) and never sees the developer's answer. We then evaluate its resolutions with a two-metric suite: (1) a developer-match LLM-as-judge implemented as a G-Eval metric and, crucially, calibrated against ConflictBench's human labels before use, and (2) a deterministic structural-validity check that uses no LLM. On a meta-evaluation of 292 human-labeled cases, the judge reaches 100% precision (zero false accepts) at 64.6% recall, so every acceptance is trustworthy and every downstream rate is a conservative lower bound. Under this validated judge, LLM solvers match the developer's own resolution on about 55% of true conflicts (conservative floor), and under a coverage-fair comparison the LLMs (55-59%) beat the strongest traditional tool (AutoMerge, 36.7%) by roughly 18-22 points; the edge comes almost entirely from coverage, not raw accuracy, since the tools abstain on 20-90% of conflicts while the LLM under forced resolution abstains on none. Finally, the LLM judge accepted 4 of the 5 resolutions that fail the deterministic structural check, evidence that structural correctness must not be delegated to an LLM.

</details>


### [127] [A comparative analysis of automated techniques for security bug report identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27893)
*Muhammad Laiq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对多种自动识别安全相关Bug报告的技术进行了系统性比较分析，评估了传统机器学习（逻辑回归、SVM、随机森林）、大型语言模型（GPT-5.2、BERT、RoBERTa）以及少样本学习框架SetFit在基准数据集上的表现。结果显示SetFit综合性能最优（F1=0.80），RoBERTa表现接近，而GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下均表现不佳。跨项目实验还发现迁移学习对数据有限的项目有益，但可能损害具有强项目特性数据的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动筛选安全相关的Bug报告耗时、易错且难以扩展，现有自动技术研究分散、评价设置不一致，导致难以比较结果并得出可靠结论，研究者和实践者缺乏明确的技术选型指导。

Method: 使用基准数据集，对逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林、OpenAI GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa和SetFit（最先进的少样本学习框架）这七种技术进行统一的比较分析，并开展了跨项目迁移学习实验。

Result: SetFit取得最佳综合性能，F1-score达0.80，在四个数据集中的三个上优于其他技术；RoBERTa表现接近SetFit；传统机器学习（特别是逻辑回归）在某些场景下仍是强基线；GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下均表现较差；跨项目迁移学习对数据有限的项目的性能有提升，但对具有强项目特性的项目可能产生性能下降。

Conclusion: 在统一的实验框架下，SetFit是当前识别安全相关Bug报告最有效的技术，但RoBERTa和逻辑回归各有其适用场景；实际应用中应根据项目数据特点选择合适的技术，并谨慎使用跨项目迁移学习。

Abstract: Timely identification of security-related bug reports is essential to minimize the window of vulnerabilities in software systems. Manually screening incoming bug reports to identify security-related issues is time-consuming, error-prone, and non-scalable for large-scale software systems. Thus, a variety of automatic techniques, including traditional machine learning (ML) techniques and large language models, have been proposed to facilitate this task. However, the literature remains fragmented. Most studies introduce or optimize a particular technique and evaluate it against a limited set of baselines, often under different experimental setups. As a result, it is difficult to compare their results and draw reliable conclusions about the effectiveness of existing approaches, leaving researchers and practitioners without clear guidance on which techniques are most suitable for the task.
  To address this gap, we conducted a comparative analysis of several promising automated techniques to identify security-related bug reports using benchmark datasets. We evaluated Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, Random Forest, OpenAI's GPT-5.2, BERT-base, RoBERTa, and SetFit (a state-of-the-art few-shot learning framework).
  Our results indicate that SetFit achieves the best overall performance, achieving an F1-score of 0.80 and outperforming other techniques on three of the four datasets. RoBERTa performs competitively and approaches SetFit in some projects, while traditional ML techniques, particularly Logistic Regression, remain a strong baseline in certain contexts. In contrast, GPT-5.2 performs poorly in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. In addition, cross-project experiments demonstrate that transfer learning can improve performance for projects with limited data, but may degrade results for projects with strong project-specific characteristics.

</details>


### [128] [The Case for Vibe Modeling: A Missing Step in AI-Based Trustworthy Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27923)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Michael Mittermaier,Judith Michael*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文主张在自然语言交互与代码生成之间引入"氛围建模"（vibe modeling）作为轻量级中间抽象层，以保留人类意图、支持系统行为推理，并通过学生问卷调查验证其在大模型辅助开发中的理解性、验证、信任与实用性方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的软件开发实践过度聚焦于直接生成代码，忽视了对保留人类意图和支持系统行为推理的中间表示的关注，导致理解、验证、可追溯性和信任方面存在问题。

Method: 提出vibe modeling作为自然语言交互与代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象，并通过学生调查研究，考察参与者对LLM输出理解程度、验证工作量、信任度以及vibe modeling感知有用性的看法。

Result: 研究结果旨在为未来关于通过vibe modeling实现可信赖和可解释的AI辅助软件工程研究提供信息支撑。

Conclusion: vibe modeling有望作为桥梁解决当前AI辅助开发中意图保留和可解释性不足的问题，但需进一步研究验证其在实际开发场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts from natural language prompts. While this enables rapid prototyping and lowers the barrier to software creation, it also introduces challenges related to understanding, validation, traceability, and trust. In this paper, we argue that current AI-based development practices focus too heavily on the direct generation of code and insufficiently on intermediate representations that preserve human intent and support reasoning about system behavior. We argue for vibe modeling as a lightweight intermediate abstraction between natural language interaction and code generation. To explore its potential, we present a student survey study that examines perceptions of LLM output understanding, validation effort, trust and the perceived usefulness of vibe modeling across several AI-assisted development scenarios. Our results are intended to inform future studies for trustworthy and explainable AI-based software engineering via vibe modeling.

</details>


### [129] [Specification-Guided Synthesis of Deadlock-Free Communication Protocol Refinements with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27964)
*Yang Li,Ping Hou,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Syntropy是一个结合多参与方会话类型（MPST）规范与大型语言模型（LLM）的框架，用于自动合成满足行为正确性保证的协议精化变体，在多个LLM上实现95.6%-99.5%的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式软件系统中协议精化（安全替换协议并保持正确性与兼容性）至关重要，但LLM虽在代码生成上表现优异，却缺乏产生可靠行为正确输出的机制；传统形式化规范方法（如MPST）虽提供死锁自由等严格保证，但难以自动构建协议精化。两者互补性未被充分利用。

Method: 提出Syntropy框架，将精化约束直接嵌入到生成过程中，以MPST规范为指导，结合LLM进行协议精化变体的合成，从而确保生成的变体满足行为正确性保证。

Result: 综合评估表明Syntropy在多个LLM上实现了95.6%-99.5%的有效性，同时保持较高的句法正确性，并能产生多样化、非平凡的协议精化。

Conclusion: Syntropy成功弥合了LLM的生成能力与形式化方法正确性保证之间的鸿沟，为分布式协议的精化合成提供了一种可靠且多样化的自动化方案。

Abstract: Ensuring behavioural correctness in communication protocols is a central challenge in distributed software systems, as subtle inconsistencies can lead to deadlocks. In such settings, protocol refinement - the safe substitution of a protocol that preserves correctness and compatibility with other components - is essential. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and program synthesis, yet lack mechanisms to reliably produce outputs with correct behaviour. Formal specification approaches, such as multiparty session types (MPST), offer rigorous guarantees, including deadlock freedom, but provide limited support for automatically constructing protocol refinements. In this paper, we present Syntropy, a framework for synthesising protocol refinements guided by MPST specifications and LLMs. It incorporates refinement constraints directly into the generation process, ensuring the generated variants satisfy these guarantees. Our comprehensive evaluation indicates that Syntropy achieves 95.6%-99.5% validity while maintaining high syntactic correctness, and produces diverse, non-trivial refinements across multiple LLMs.

</details>


### [130] [Advancing Awkward Arrays for High-Performance CPU and GPU Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28145)
*Ianna Osborne,Manasvi Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Awkward Array库的GPU后端最新进展，包括基于NVIDIA CCCL库的CUDA实现、优化的内存管理以及针对不规则（ragged）数组的分段归约算法，在保持Python编程模型不变的情况下显著提升了GPU吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理领域的HL-LHC分析越来越依赖加速器硬件（如GPU），但Awkward Array所处理的嵌套、变长数据结构在GPU上难以高效执行，因为涉及间接索引、分段操作和不规则内存访问模式。因此需要开发高效的GPU后端来加速这类不规则工作负载。

Method: 基于NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL)构建CUDA实现，设计了优化的内存管理策略，并实现了针对ragged数组的分段归约算法。同时建立了自动化验证框架，用于确保GPU实现与现有CPU行为的正确性。

Result: 通过将CPU、CuPy和CUDA实现进行基准对比，展示了GPU后端在保持现有Python编程模型的前提下，显著提升了不规则工作负载的GPU吞吐量。

Conclusion: 这些GPU后端开发使得Awkward Array能够在保持Python编程模型的同时，有效利用GPU加速嵌套变长数据的处理，为HL-LHC等大规模高能物理分析提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Awkward Array is a Python library for representing and processing nested, variable-length data that is widely used in high-energy physics. As HL-LHC analyses increasingly rely on accelerator hardware, efficient execution of irregular workloads has become essential. While dense numerical arrays map naturally to GPUs, nested and variable-length data structures remain significantly more difficult to accelerate because they require indirect indexing, segmented operations, and irregular memory access patterns. We present recent developments in the Awkward Array GPU backend, including CUDA implementations built on NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL), optimized memory management, and segmented reduction algorithms for ragged arrays. These developments preserve the existing Python programming model while substantially improving GPU throughput on irregular workloads. We describe the backend architecture, automated validation framework, and benchmark results comparing CPU, CuPy, and CUDA implementations.

</details>


### [131] [Integrating AI into Requirements Quality Learning in Software Engineering Education: A TPACK-Guided Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28176)
*Hansika Ekanayake Mudiyanselage,Rohan Jai Dharmaraj,Malik Abdul Sami,Zheying Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用混合方法（N=100，72份提交分析），探讨TPACK框架指导下的多智能体AI工具在硕士级需求工程（RE）课程作业中的整合效果。结果表明，学生以选择性方式使用AI（主要辅助分析与评估而非自动化），其中结构具体化的质量维度（如价值表达、可测试性）对齐改进最明显，而可协商性效果不一。学生表现出条件性信任、主动细化与质量标准意识提升，同时存在适度的可用性挑战。TPACK引导的脚手架可将AI能力与教学目标和RE内容对齐，为RE教育中的负责任AI整合提供设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程实践中迅速普及，但缺乏教学法上有根据的AI整合方法，尤其在需求工程这类概念密集型课程中。本研究旨在填补该空白，探索如何在RE教育中结构化地整合AI工具，使其辅助而非替代学生的学习。

Method: 采用TPACK（技术-教学-内容知识）框架指导多智能体AI工具在硕士级RE作业（需求质量分析）中的整合设计；使用混合方法设计，共100名学生参与，分析72份提交作业；通过定量与定性相结合的方式考察作业设计的结构性如何塑造学生的AI使用行为、对用户故事质量标准的理解以及对其优缺点感知的影响。

Result: 学生主要将AI用作分析与评估的辅助工具而非自动化替代；结构具体化的质量维度（如价值表达、可测试性）的对齐改进最为显著，而可协商性维度呈现混合效果；学生表现出条件性信任、主动精炼行为以及对质量标准的认知提升，同时报告了适度的可用性挑战。

Conclusion: TPACK引导的脚手架设计能够有效将AI的工具性能力与教学目标及RE领域内容对齐，为在需求工程教育中负责任地整合AI提供了可操作的设计指南，也为其他概念密集型软件工程课程中的AI整合提供了参考框架。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in software engineering (SE) practice creates a need for pedagogically grounded approaches to AI integration in SE education, especially in conceptually intensive subjects such as requirements engineering (RE). This study examines a TPACK-guided integration of a multi-agent AI tool into a master-level RE assignment on requirements quality analysis. Using a mixed-methods design (N=100; 72 submissions analysed), we examine how structured assignment design shaped students' AI use, affected their understanding of user story quality criteria, and influenced their perceptions of AI's benefits and limitations. Results show that students used the AI tool selectively, mainly as support for analysis and evaluation rather than automation. Alignment improvements were most evident for structurally concrete requirements quality dimensions, such as value articulation and testability, while negotiability showed mixed effects. Students reported conditional trust, active refinement, and increased awareness of quality criteria, alongside moderate usability challenges. The findings show that TPACK-guided scaffolding can align AI affordances with pedagogical goals and RE content, offering design guidance for responsible AI integration in RE education.

</details>


### [132] [Agentic Metaverse Services: A New As-a-Service Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28242)
*Xiaofei Xu,Quan Z. Sheng,Zhongjie Wang,Boualem Benatallah,Xiao Wang,Ruipeng Han*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文概述了生成式人工智能（GenAI）驱动的智能体与服务业态演进，提出了元宇宙环境下的智能体服务新范式——智能体元宇宙服务（AMServ）及其实现方式Meta-AaaS，并探讨了其形式、特征、应用案例及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI正在重构数字虚拟世界，智能体从对话式聊天机器人向智能体AI（Agentic AI）演进，催生了智能体即服务（AaaS）的新型服务模式。将智能体服务与元宇宙结合，能够为元宇宙业务处理提供新的服务形态。然而，目前对AMServ和Meta-AaaS的系统性研究尚缺乏，需要对其演化、特征、原理和应用进行全面的梳理与展望。

Method: 本文采用综述与概念化研究方法：(1) 梳理GenAI赋能下智能体与服务业的演化历程及新特征；(2) 揭示智能体服务在元宇宙环境中的角色与原则；(3) 提出AMServ与Meta-AaaS的形式、特征和原理；(4) 讨论典型应用实例；(5) 指出未来趋势与研究方向。

Result: 提出了AMServ（智能体元宇宙服务）作为元宇宙业务处理的新服务形态，以及Meta-AaaS作为实现AMServ的新范式；系统化了智能体服务在元宇宙中的角色定位、运行原则与实现框架，并给出了典型应用示例。

Conclusion: AMServ与Meta-AaaS作为GenAI时代新兴的服务范式，将为人类社会的元宇宙化服务带来重大机遇，并推动未来新兴服务产业的快速发展，具有广阔的研究与实践前景。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is reconstructing the digital virtual world, upgrading agents through enhancing their abilities in autonomous learning, multi-modal interaction, content generation, and collaborative decision-making. In particular, the shift from conversational chatbots to agentic AI, the most recent significant technical breakthrough of GenAI, has brought a new form of services, agentic services and Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), in which the agent's abilities are encapsulated, such as perception, decision-making, execution, collaboration, and content generation, to provide the customized agent services to users. The metaverse is a virtual ecosystem for human life, work, creation, and entertainment, supported by the new generation of digital technologies. Through combining agentic services and the metaverse, an Agentic Metaverse Service, denoted as AMServ, is produced for metaverse business processing, as a new form of metaverse service. The AaaS in the metaverse environment, denoted as Meta-AaaS, as an approach to realize AMServ, has become a new paradigm of agentic services and service computing. This paper overviews the evolution and new features of agents and services empowered by GenAI, reveals the roles and principles of agentic services in the metaverse environment, presents the forms, characteristics, and principles of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, discusses the typical application examples of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, and finally points out the new tendencies and research directions of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS. The AMServ and the Meta-AaaS will bring great opportunities to human society and services in the AI era, and promote the rapid development of emerging service industries in the future.

</details>


### [133] [Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28271)
*Andras Ferenczi,Jordan Docherty,Mariya Bessonov,Matthew Findlay,Krishna Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"Locksmith Loop"方法，一种基于代理的测试合成方法，用于验证COBOL到Java的程序迁移。通过在两个运行时环境中迭代执行实证搜索和保留奇偶性的突变，穿透程序分支并识别阻塞段，在三个案例研究中（代码行数430到4,114），显著提升了分支覆盖率（内部生产级程序达91.90%），且所有接受测试的Java代码均通过确定性奇偶校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL程序向Java迁移需要大量测试以确保功能正确，但由于缺乏测试数据和难以验证所有边界情况，这一过程常常受阻。需要一个系统化的方法来生成有效测试数据并验证迁移代码的正确性。

Method: 提出代理型测试合成方法"Locksmith Loop"：(1) 准备两个运行时环境，分别对COBOL源程序和生成的Java目标程序进行mock插桩，在非大型机商品硬件上执行；(2) 迭代代理循环，先进行Witness Search穿透程序分支，再进行保持奇偶性的突变；(3) 当到达路由边界时，分析器识别"Locked Paragraph"，即阻止更深层探索的条件。

Result: 在三个COBOL-Java案例研究中（涵盖两个开源程序和内部生产级COBOL程序，代码行数430-4,114行），Locksmith持续改善覆盖率，超越输入搜索的瓶颈，在两个开源程序中接近完全覆盖，内部生产级程序达到91.90%分支覆盖率。所有接受的测试用例中，生成的Java都通过确定性奇偶校验与COBOL参考匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了利用确定性预言机验证代理编码输出的新型方法，为COBOL到Java迁移的测试验证提供了系统化的解决方案，并证明了代理式测试合成在大型程序迁移验证中的有效性。

Abstract: Migration of legacy COBOL programs to Java requires extensive testing to ensure correct functionality. This effort is often complicated by the lack of test data and the difficulty of validating all corner cases. In this paper we propose a novel agentic test-synthesis method, the "Locksmith Loop," which is initiated by preparing two runtime environments: the COBOL source and the generated Java target are each instrumented with mocks and executed off-mainframe on commodity hardware, then an iterative agentic loop performs Witness Search over input mocks to penetrate program branches, followed by parity-preserving mutations. When routing boundaries are reached, an analyzer identifies a Locked Paragraph: a condition preventing deeper exploration. Across three COBOL-Java case studies, spanning two open-source programs and one internal production-like COBOL program and ranging from 430 to 4,114 source lines, Locksmith consistently improved coverage beyond input-search plateaus, reaching nearly complete coverage on the two open-source programs and 91.90% branch coverage on the internal production-like COBOL program. The generated Java matched the COBOL reference under deterministic parity checks in all accepted test cases. Through these findings we demonstrate, to the best of our knowledge, a novel approach for validating agentic coding output using a deterministic oracle.

</details>


### [134] [From Textual Requirements to Microservice Architectures - A Comprehensive Evaluation of LLM-Based Design Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28307)
*Danyllo Albuquerque,José Renan,Guillermo Rodríguez,Guillermo Rodríguez,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França,Mirko Perkusich,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法（定量对比+专家盲评）研究OpenAI o3能否仅从自然语言需求中生成微服务架构。零样本（ZS）与少样本（FS）提示分别应用于Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统。结果显示FS提示显著提升服务识别一致性（F1从0.79升至0.97）和通信恢复一致性（F1从0.61升至0.82），专家评估也认为FS输出更模块化、连贯且合理。研究局限在于结果仅限于两个小型系统和特定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构已成为现代化单体系统的主流方案，但服务识别仍以人工为主且极具挑战。现有分解方法多为代码驱动，在仅有文本需求的前期设计阶段难以应用。尽管LLM取得进展，但缺乏其能否从自然语言需求综合出完整微服务架构（含服务定义与交互）的实证证据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，使用OpenAI o3在零样本（ZS）和少样本（FS）两种提示策略下，对Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统各执行一次生成。架构评估分两路：（i）与参考架构对比，用精确率、召回率和F1分数衡量服务识别与通信恢复；（ii）由专家盲评正确性、完整性、模块化与合理性，并综合开放式反馈。

Result: FS提示下服务识别F1达到0.97（ZS为0.79）；通信恢复更具挑战性，ZS产生密集架构但召回高精确低（F1=0.61），FS提升至F1=0.82并减少不支持的依赖。专家评估佐证了量化结果，FS架构被认为更模块化、连贯和合理。

Conclusion: OpenAI o3在示例提示（few-shot）引导下展现出从需求驱动生成微服务架构的潜力，但结果具有模型特异性和上下文依赖性，不能推广为模型无关的普适结论，需在更大规模系统上进一步验证。

Abstract: Microservice architectures have become dominant for modernizing monolithic systems, yet identifying appropriate services remains challenging and largely manual. Existing decomposition approaches are predominantly code-centric, limiting applicability in early design stages where only textual requirements are available. Despite advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), limited empirical evidence exists on their ability to synthesize complete microservice architectures from natural-language requirements, including service definitions and inter-service interactions. This study investigates whether an LLM can bridge requirements engineering and architectural design, generating architectures solely from textual requirements and evaluating structural agreement and perceived quality of results. We conduct a mixed-method study using OpenAI o3 under zero-shot (ZS) and few-shot (FS) prompting across two systems (Bookstore, PetClinic), one execution per system/condition. Architectures are evaluated through (i) comparison with reference architectures using precision, recall, and F1-score for service identification and communication recovery, and (ii) a blinded expert assessment of correctness, completeness, modularity, and plausibility, plus open feedback synthesis. OpenAI o3 identifies services with higher agreement under FS prompting (F1 = 0.79 for ZS versus = 0.97 for FS). Communication recovery is more challenging: ZS produces dense architectures with high recall but low precision (F1 = 0.61), while FS improves agreement, reaching F1 = 0.82 and reducing unsupported dependencies. Expert evaluation corroborates these results, with FS architectures perceived as more modular, coherent, and plausible than ZS outputs. OpenAI o3 shows potential for requirements-driven synthesis when guided by exemplar prompting. Results are model- and context-specific from two small systems, not model-independent proof.

</details>


### [135] [Structural Validation of LLM-Generated Microservice Decompositions Using Source-Code Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28331)
*Daniel Silva,Renan Alves,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM（OpenAI o3）生成的微服务分解方案在结构依赖保真度上的表现，提出基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，并发现控制类到服务映射覆盖率后，零样本与少样本提示策略在结构遵从性上表现相当（TPD值：PetClinic 68.0%，Bookstore 83.3%），提示了映射覆盖率控制对消除方法论偏差的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能从文本需求生成语义上合理的微服务分解方案，但这些方案是否保留源代码中已实现的结构依赖关系尚不明确。现有评估缺乏对结构依赖保真度的系统验证，且不同提示策略之间的差异可能被映射覆盖率差异所混淆。

Method: 以PetClinic和Bookstore两个系统为对象，使用OpenAI o3生成微服务分解方案；构建基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道；比较零样本和少样本两种提示策略；采用依赖保留（TPD）和依赖违反（TVD）指标度量结构遵从性；通过鲁棒性分析控制类到服务映射覆盖率的差异，并进行归一化处理。

Result: 归一化后，两种提示策略产生的结构遵从性在统计上等价，TPD值分别为PetClinic 68.0%和Bookstore 83.3%。提示策略间的表面差异在控制映射覆盖率后消失，说明差异源于方法论偏差而非真实的架构质量差异。

Conclusion: 对LLM生成的分解方案进行结构评估时，必须显式控制映射覆盖率，否则提示策略间的表现差异可能反映的是方法论偏差而非架构质量。这为LLM驱动的软件现代化评估提供了重要的方法论启示。

Abstract: Decomposing monolithic systems into microservices is a key activity in software modernization. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate semantically plausible decompositions from textual requirements, it remains unclear whether these proposals preserve the structural dependencies implemented in the source code. This paper evaluates the structural adherence of microservice decompositions generated by OpenAI o3 for the PetClinic and Bookstore systems. We propose an automated validation pipeline based on static dependency analysis and compare zero-shot and few-shot prompting using dependency preservation (TPD) and dependency violation (TVD) metrics. A robustness analysis was conducted to control for differences in class-to-service mapping coverage. After normalization, both prompting strategies produced equivalent structural adherence, achieving TPD values of 68.0% (PetClinic) and 83.3% (Bookstore). The findings demonstrate that structural evaluations of LLM-generated decompositions should explicitly control for mapping coverage, as apparent differences between prompting strategies may otherwise reflect methodological bias rather than genuine architectural quality.

</details>


### [136] [Demystifying Solana Bots: From GitHub Blueprints to On-Chain Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28424)
*Xiaoye Zheng,Yujing Chen,Minghao Wu,David Lo,Difan Xie,Daoyuan Wu,Xiaohu Yang,Zhiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Solana区块链上的机器人（bot）进行了大规模实证研究，结合586个GitHub代码仓库和200个链上机器人地址（超过4400万笔交易），提出了基于实现的Solana机器人分类体系（5大领域15个类别），识别出五阶段共享操作流水线，并揭示了机器人在不同交易平台和资产上的链上交易行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana作为高吞吐量、低交易费用的区块链平台，容易受到机器人垃圾交易和金融利用的影响。然而，现有研究主要集中在系统性能、智能合约安全和特定链上现象，缺乏对Solana机器人在实践中具体实现方式及其链上可观察执行特征的系统性理解。

Method: 采用大规模实证研究方法，从两个互补视角收集数据：（i）从GitHub收集586个机器人代码仓库；（ii）在Solana链上识别200个机器人地址，涵盖超过4400万笔链上交易。通过对代码实现和链上行为进行综合分析，归纳出Solana机器人的分类体系、操作流水线和交易行为模式。

Result: 研究得出三项主要发现：（1）建立了基于实现细节的Solana机器人分类体系，包含5大领域（如交易操作、MEV、链上分析）共15个类别；（2）识别出机器人实现中普遍共享的五阶段操作流水线；（3）揭示了Solana机器人在不同交易平台和资产类别上链上交易行为存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，论文提出了未来研究方向，并为在Solana区块链上构建和运营机器人提供了实践建议。

Abstract: Solana is an emerging blockchain platform designed for high throughput and low transaction fees, making it inexpensive to submit transactions at scale and, consequently, increasing exposure to bot spamming and related financial exploitation. Solana bots are typically off-chain software systems that operate in a competitive on-chain execution environment by constructing and submitting transactions, and the bot-related transactions on the decentralized exchanges exceed 250 million dollars in daily trading volume in January 2026. Prior studies on Solana have examined system performance, smart-contract security, and specific on-chain phenomena. However, we still lack a systematic understanding of what Solana bots implement in practice and how these implementations manifest as observable on-chain execution fingerprints. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale empirical study of Solana bots from two complementary views: (i) 586 bot repositories collected from GitHub, and (ii) 200 bot addresses on Solana, with over 44 million on-chain transactions. Our study derives an implementation-grounded taxonomy of Solana bots comprising 15 categories grouped into five domains (e.g., Trading Operations, MEV, and On-chain Analytics), identifies a largely shared five-stage operational pipeline manifested in bot implementations, and uncovers systematic variation in on-chain trading behaviors of Solana bots across diverse trading platforms and assets. Based on our findings, we highlight future research directions, and provide recommendations for building and operating bots on the Solana blockchain.

</details>


### [137] [Emerging Challenges in Threat Modeling for GenAI-Augmented Systems: A View from the Trenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28431)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Manish Mahesh Kumar,Nohemí Villarreal,Pankaj Pantel,Immo Brueggemann,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次在中小型企业环境中对GenAI感知的威胁建模方法进行了探索性评估，通过快速文献综述选出三种方法并应用于包含GenAI增强系统的工业案例，揭示不同方法的威胁识别差异以及它们对GenAI特定风险类别的有限支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能在软件系统中日益普及，传统的威胁建模方法（如STRIDE）无法充分评估GenAI特有的安全风险，而现有研究缺乏在中小型企业环境下对这些新方法的实证评估。

Method: 采用快速文献综述筛选相关的GenAI感知威胁建模技术，并将筛选出的三种方法系统地应用于一个包含GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究中，同时收集从业者对方法可用性和集成的看法。

Result: 各技术识别的威胁存在差异，且在软件供应链和以人为中心的安全问题等GenAI特定风险类别上的支持有限；此外，从业者报告了这些方法在中小型企业开发工作流中的可用性、感知工作量及采用挑战。

Conclusion: 现有GenAI感知威胁建模方法在中小型企业中的应用仍存在明显不足，尤其是在GenAI特定风险覆盖和实际工作流集成方面，需要进一步改进以满足中小企业需求。

Abstract: Threat modeling remains a central task in secure software engineering, as it enables the identification of security issues from system architectures. As Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) becomes increasingly pervasive across software systems, traditional threat modeling methods (e.g., STRIDE) are insufficient to assess emerging GenAI-specific risks. In this work, we present the first results from an exploratory assessment of GenAI-aware threat modeling methods in a Small and Medium Enterprise (SME) setting. For this, we conducted a rapid literature review to select relevant techniques and systematically applied three shortlisted methods to an industrial case study involving a GenAI-augmented system. The results highlight differences in the threats identified by each technique and reveal limited support for certain GenAI-specific risk categories, particularly those related to software supply chains and human-centered security issues. We further report practitioners' perceptions of the usability and integration of these methods in SME development workflows, including their perceived effort and adoption challenges.

</details>


### [138] [CoGate: Confidence-Gated Co-Decoding for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28529)
*Minghao Hu,Lannan Luo,Allen Roush,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGate，一种基于置信度门控的协同解码方法，用于提升大语言模型代码生成的安全性。CoGate根据安全专家模型的置信度动态控制其对协同解码过程的影响，解决了现有协同解码方法忽略专家置信度而可能被误导的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成时可能产生不安全的程序。协解码方法（将小专家模型与目标模型结合）是解决该问题的重要方案，但现有协同解码的接受规则未考虑专家模型的置信度。当安全专家因未见模式或分布外（OOD）上下文而不够自信时，其指导可能具有误导性。

Method: 提出CoGate（Confidence-Gated co-decoding），一种置信度门控的协同解码方法。该方法基于专家模型的置信度来控制专家在协同解码过程中对目标模型的影响程度，从而在专家不自信时降低其干扰。

Result: 在多个LLM后端（CodeGen、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen-Coder、StarCoder）和多个代码生成基准（HumanEval、security suite、CWEval）上评估。CoGate在多个基准上优于现有协同解码方法CoSec+，在CWEval上实现了高达12.6%的Func-Sec@10提升。

Conclusion: CoGate通过基于置信度的门控机制，能够有效提升协同解码在代码安全生成方面的性能，特别是在分布外上下文下避免被低置信度专家的误导性指导所影响。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used for code generation, but they can also produce insecure programs due to patterns learned from their pretraining data. Decoding-time steering has become an important solution to this problem: a small expert model is combined with the target model at each step to generate more secure code, which is referred to as co-decoding. However, the acceptance rule for existing co-decoding approaches does not consider the expert model's confidence. When the security expert is unconfident due to unseen patterns or out-of-distribution (OOD) contexts, its guidance can therefore be misleading. To address the challenge, we propose CoGate, a confidence-gated co-decoding approach that controls the expert's influence on the co-decoding process based on its confidence. We implement our approach and evaluate it across multiple LLM backends (CodeGen, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen-Coder, StarCoder) on several code generation benchmarks (HumanEval, security suite, and CWEval). Our approach outperforms existing co-decoding methods (CoSec+) across multiple benchmarks, achieving up to a 12.6% gain of Func-Sec@10 on CWEval.

</details>


### [139] [PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28587)
*Manyi Wang,Junjielong Xu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SWE-bench类基准中PR-Issue错配问题，发现13.6%实例存在五种模式下的十一类细粒度错配场景，并提出PAIChecker多智能体系统进行检测，在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上分别达到92.12%和91.67%的二元准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench类基准广泛用于评估LLM的问题解决能力，但其PR-Issue配对常因大型代码库的复杂性而错配，影响基准可靠性和评估准确性。现有方法缺乏对此错配问题的系统性研究。

Method: 提出PAIChecker多智能体系统，采用三阶段设计：特定模式识别、跨智能体标签合成、代码级验证，实现更准确、可泛化且渐进验证的错配检测。

Result: 在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上，PAIChecker在四种LLM骨干下均取得最佳性能，二元准确率分别达到92.12%和91.67%。

Conclusion: PAIChecker能够有效、可扩展地检测SWE-bench类基准中的PR-Issue错配问题，为未来可靠构建此类基准提供了支持。

Abstract: SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [140] [Change2Task: From Repository Changes to Executable Coding Agent Tasks and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28591)
*Haomin Qi,Xingliang Wang,Xuanqi Gao,Baihui Sang,Xin Zhang,Minghua Ma,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Change2Task系统，从仓库历史中挖掘已合并的拉取请求，将其转化为在健康现代代码版本上可验证的编码智能体任务，支持训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展编码智能体需要持续供给可执行的训练和评估数据。每个任务需耦合真实软件状态、规格说明、开发工具和可靠验证，而现有数据构造方式（如直接基于拉取请求）在现代仓库修订版本上无法有效复用历史任务，导致任务供给不足。

Method: Change2Task基于仓库历史，将已合并的拉取请求转换为健康现代修订版本上可验证的任务。核心流程包括：将历史证据与现代代码对齐；通过补丁回退（Patch Reversal）、代码映射（Code Mapping）或智能体重建（Agent Reconstruction）重构任务状态；并从健康基线到任务状态再到恢复状态验证整个生命周期。系统支持五类编码智能体任务：缺陷修复、功能新增、测试生成、API迁移、安全修复。

Result: 从1,130个可构造的源码变更中，Change2Task在五类任务家族上达到79.6%的验证通过任务构造成功率。在匹配候选集上，比基于拉取请求的构造基线多恢复29.2%的验证通过任务。历史案例与重建案例在智能体评估中实现最高98.0%的结果一致性，且复用现代基线使完整流水线测量开销降低10.8%。

Conclusion: Change2Task通过从维护良好的仓库环境中派生出多个基于开发者证据的可验证任务，为编码智能体训练与评估提供了可执行数据，同时减少了重复环境搭建、存储和任务构造成本。

Abstract: Scaling coding agents requires a continuing supply of executable data for training, benchmarking, and continuous evaluation. Each task must couple a realistic software state with a specification, development tools, and reliable verification.
